- **[qa-test-strategy:qa-09] MobileStore.submit/load are untested despite an existing URLProtocol harness** — `ios/SocraticTrade/MobileStore.swift:358` · improvement · iOS · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: submit() is the path every approve/reject/stop/activate tap takes (idempotency key, busy dedupe, pendingAccountId, proposal feedback) and load() decides stale-snapshot fallback and 401 handling; none of that has a test, so a regression shows up as double-submitted commands or a blank Home on the phone.
  - Fix: Add ios/SocraticTradeTests/MobileStoreTests.swift using the existing MobileTestURLProtocol: (1) submit sends the Idempotency-Key header and refuses a second submit while busy; (2) submit with an unadvertised command sets `error` and does not hit the network; (3) load() on a 500 keeps the cached snapshot and sets snapshotLoadFailed; (4) load() on 401 after init routes through a…

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-11] No phone type-scale bump: 412 uses of 11px and 185 of 12.5px text render at desktop sizes on a 6-inch screen** — `app/console/console.css:90` · improvement · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Body copy on proposals, hints, table cells and chips is overwhelmingly `sm`/`xs`; on a phone held at arm's length 11-12.5px is below iOS/Android body defaults (17pt/16sp) and forces pinch-zoom on the very screens the owner triages from a phone. Because everything is tokenized, this is a one-block fix with no per-component churn.
  - Fix: Add `@media (max-width: 640px) { .console-root { --con-fs-xs: 12px; --con-fs-sm: 14px; --con-fs-base: 15px; --con-fs-md: 16px; } }` (and let 2xs stay for captions), then spot-check the chrome bar/tab bar which use literal 10px.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-12] Phone chrome-bar controls are 32px (avatar, ⌘K trigger, run-state chip) — known follow-up still open** — `app/console/components/chrome.tsx:900` · improvement · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-04 P2 (32px avatar)
  - Why: The three secondary bar controls sit at 32px next to a 44px Stop button; the StateChip (chrome.tsx:300-315, py-1 with 11px/10px stacked text) is ~30px tall and opens the money-relevant ControlSheet. The 32px avatar was already flagged (2026-08-04 P2 "Settings touch targets (32px avatar)") and remains.
  - Fix: Give these a `.con-bar-ctl` hook and include it in the `(pointer: coarse)` 44px block (visual size can stay 32 via inner element); fix the stale 44px comment.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-13] Overlay close/pin/row targets under 44px: sheet X is 18px, drawer X 32px, More-sheet pins 36px and rows ~35px, scan star 28px, expand toggle text-only** — `app/console/ui/sheet.tsx:157` · improvement · Mobile web · effort S · CONFIRMED
  - Why: On phones these are the primary ways out of a modal (no Escape key) and the primary way to pin tabs / watch a symbol / expand a proposal's full reasoning; an 18px X in the corner of a bottom sheet is easy to miss and mis-taps land on the header.
  - Fix: Standardize an `IconTarget` wrapper (44x44 hit area, 18px glyph) used by Sheet, TabsSheet, SymbolDrawer; add `@media (pointer:coarse){ .con-nav-item{min-height:44px} .ac-expand-toggle{min-height:44px} }` and bump WatchButton to h-9/w-9 with a coarse floor.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-14] Watchlist has no phone card variant — a 5-column table with row actions in the last column scrolls its Alert/Delete buttons off-screen** — `app/console/watchlist/page.tsx:227` · improvement · Mobile web · effort M · CONFIRMED
  - Why: Orders (orders/page.tsx:402), Positions (positions.tsx:158) and Scan (scan-table.tsx:505) all switch to stacked cards below lg; Watchlist does not, so at 360-430px the ghost `Alert`/trash buttons (watchlist/page.tsx:262-286) sit past the right edge with no scroll affordance and 28px-tall `size="sm"` targets. Watchlist is a natural phone screen (glance at prices, arm an alert).
  - Fix: Add the same `lg:hidden` card list (symbol + price + armed count + Alert/Remove buttons at ≥40px) and hide the table below lg; while at it add a subtle right-edge fade to `.overflow-x-auto` tables that still exist on Results.

- **[mobile-web-ux:mweb-15] Home on a phone buries portfolio value / day P&L under the readiness hero, strategy bar and "Latest strategy run" list** — `app/console/page.tsx:217` · improvement · Mobile web · effort M · CONFIRMED · prior 2026-08-04 R6
  - Why: On phone the grid collapses to one column so the first thing after the strategy bar is a list of last-run proposals; equity, day P&L, cash and next-run appear only after scrolling past them. A trader opening the site on a phone wants the number first. Prior review R6 ("Ready-state Home density: equity/P&L hero") is still open.
  - Fix: Below xl, render a compact 2x2 stat strip (Portfolio value, Day P&L, Cash/BP, Deployed today) directly under the strategy bar (reuse the existing Stat components), and collapse Coach/Framework cards by default on phones.

## 6. Surface-By-Surface Digest

### Desktop Web
- Home: dead Approve on latest-run rows + wrong tones (`dweb-01`), click-only rows (`a11y-01`, `dweb-18`), no attention/equity hero and duplicated cadence/spend blocks (`dweb-03`), Coach card with dead quick-action chips (`dweb-07`), positions blotter without last price / day change / exit action (`dweb-08`).
- Navigation and IA: Decisions route orphaned from nav while the proposal lifecycle is spread over five surfaces (`dweb-05`); default 404 inside the console (LIVE-02); command palette lacks ticker lookup / STOP / account switch (`dweb-11`); bare `r` fires a run (`dweb-02`); freshness strip only at document end (`dweb-04`).
- Configure pages: three save models across Strategy / Guardrails / Connections (`dweb-09`); Settings tab strip overflows with no affordance (LIVE-13); Guardrails header says "hard execution constraints" and the Autonomy card contradicts itself (LIVE-04); Proposals page refuses reject while stopped (LIVE-05).
- Accessibility: contrast failures on tone chips + Stop/Start (`a11y-02`), no skip link (`a11y-05`), same document title on every route (`a11y-04`), sheet Escape not stack-aware (`a11y-03`), toasts auto-dismiss without pause (`a11y-06`), ~147 explanations only in `title=` (`a11y-07`).
- Performance: Connections bundle 1.05 MB (`dweb-01` perf), dashboard snapshot is a "load the universe" payload every 15 s (`dweb-03` perf), 3× re-render per poll (`dweb-06` perf), Sentry replay + `motion` shipped everywhere (`dweb-09`, `dweb-10`), HTML→JS→/api/dashboard strict waterfall (`dweb-11` perf), TickerLogo requests dark then light (`dweb-07` perf).

### Mobile Web (phone-width site)
- Chrome bar over budget since "Stop Agent" (`mweb-01`, LIVE-11); iOS Safari tint follows OS not the light theme (`mweb-02`); auto-zoom on 11–15px inputs (`mweb-05`); hard-coded bottom offsets collide with the tab bar (`mweb-06`); sheets do not lock scroll (`mweb-07`) and ignore browser history (`mweb-08`); typed-confirm inputs vs the keyboard (`mweb-09`); 36×20 toggles (`mweb-10`); no phone type-scale bump (`mweb-11`); many sub-44px targets (`mweb-12`, `mweb-13`); Watchlist has no card variant (`mweb-14`); Home buries portfolio value / day P&L (`mweb-15`); "System" theme lost on reload (`mweb-03`); ⌘K theme toggle inert (`mweb-04`); "#autonomy" literal (LIVE-12).

### iOS
- Correctness: sign-out leaves cached snapshot (`ios-engineering:ios-01`); silent errors in Settings sheet (`ios-hig-ux:ios-01`); approve "succeeded" on blocked (`api-02`/`tsx-03`); stop-loss "—" (`api-01`); scan timeout below server budget (`api-03`); 3-minute staleness gate (`ios-engineering:ios-03`); cold-start push tap dropped (`ios-05`); all-or-nothing decode (`api-07`); iOS drops `benchmarkUnavailable`/`unverifiedFlows` and always claims cash flows neutralized (`perf-13`); Insights hardcodes SPY (`ios-hig-ux:ios-05`); "1 Proposals" (LIVE-15).
- Product / HIG: Coach markdown raw (`ios-02`); Suggested Draft cannot be staged (`ios-03`); Scan header vs stacked rows (`ios-04`); Activity is fills + own commands only (`ios-06`); system green/orange fail contrast (`ios-07`); destructive-role alert on brokerage switch (`ios-08` — owner-philosophy); first-run dead end (`ios-09`); positions without P&L (`ios-10`, LIVE-14); no success acknowledgement / haptics for money actions (`ios-11`); push permission prompt without pre-prompt (LIVE-16); loosening rituals (LIVE-17 — owner decision); tab/parity mismatch with web and no Strategy/Connections/Settings on the phone (LIVE-18); "Live" pill on every live proposal (`copy-12`); universe copy points at a non-existent Strategy page (`copy-04`).
- Engineering / ship: Swift tests never run in CI or before TestFlight (`qa-01`, `ios-09` eng), Swift build not a merge gate (`qa-02`), `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` missing (`ios-10` eng), MobileStore submit/load untested (`qa-09`), verifier-added: `.serverError` releases the command attempt on 502/503/504 (possible double submit) and a single failed background reload trips `snapshotLoadFailed`.

### Public Site
- Over-promised controls (`dpub-03`), untrue `/framework` invariant (`dpub-04`), Terms not updated for data pooling (`dpub-05`), `/access-denied` dead end for every Auth.js error (`dpub-02`), deep-link lost on session expiry (`dpub-01`), no two-space gaps anywhere (`dpub-06`), sentence-case CTAs "Request access" / "Decision framework" (LIVE), no mention of the iOS app on any growth page (verifier-added), login page prints env-var names when auth is unconfigured (LIVE).

### API + Backend
- Money path: `tsx-01`, `tsx-02`, `tsx-04`, `tsx-05`, `tsx-06` (tradability stub), `tsx-08`, `tsx-09`, `tsx-10` (approve starved by autonomous run), `tsx-11` (hard-coded 5% headroom gate with no policy toggle — owner-philosophy), `tsx-12` ("safe to retry" with no retry path), `tsx-14` (remediation-deferred spam).
- Accounting: `perf-01`…`perf-14` (see §1 item 4; plus HWM not flow-adjusted / UTC day `perf-08`, first-partial-exit grading `perf-09`, 20 bps haircut on real Alpaca-paper fills `perf-10`, closing fills without app lot dropped `perf-12`, broker-authoritative equity/cash ledger implemented but unwired `perf-14`).
- Reliability: `berel-01`…`berel-10` (FTS full scan, sync FTS mirrors, no broker timeout, no tick re-entrancy guard, no event-loop-lag instrumentation, all CPU-heavy RAG on the serving thread, resume slices re-parse artifacts).
- LLM: `llm-01`…`llm-16` (strict schema, wrong account in Red Team, macro pruning, auto-applied directives, half-approve without haircut, full Green content re-sent to Red, json_object transports off-schema, dead-model list, late responses unmetered, native-provider Coach routing, `MockLLM` semantic gate in production, no `usage.cost` capture).
- Data: `mdi-01`…`mdi-13` (Friday freeze, 3-day bars, fabricated asOf ×2, ask-as-price, VWAP yesterday, screener asOf sentence, preferreds/units in dynamic universes, mixed volume semantics, no early-close calendar, unlabeled adjustment mixing, Yahoo v7 crumb, UTC day keys).
- API contract: `api-01`…`api-11` (nested `riskRules`, approve success lie, scan timeout, full StrategyResult stored in `mobile_commands`, FIFO behind run_once, no generated fixtures, all-or-nothing decode, zod unused / four error envelopes).
- Security: `sec-01`…`sec-06`.  Ops: `sre-01`…`sre-14`.  QA: `qa-01`…`qa-17`.

### Cross-Surface Parity
- One run state with three names and one stop control with four (`copy-07`); "Approvals" vs "Proposals" (`copy-05`); "Realized/Unrealized P&L" mean three different things on Results, Home and iOS (`perf-05`); win rate "0%" for zero closed lots on web + iOS (`perf-06`); overdue next run never flagged on either surface (`sre-11`); tabs differ web-mobile vs iOS (LIVE-18); Run once not stamped with account + LIVE|PAPER on either (2026-08-04 R1, still open).

## 9. Strengths — Do Not Churn

Deduped lightly; each lane's own words, prefixed with the lane.

- [ios-hig-ux] Two-space sentence rule is applied consistently across every user-facing Swift string (a grep for single-space sentence gaps in string literals returns only 'Avg. Return') — do not churn.

# Part II — Adversarial Re-Verification, Gap Coverage, And The Deduped Fix Plan (2026-08-19)

Part I ended with an honest weakness: the first verification round confirmed 336 of 336 findings and refuted none, which is evidence of leniency rather than of correctness — and several lanes admitted they had only grepped surfaces they should have read.  Part II closes both gaps.  A second round of skeptics was told to **refute** every P1, five new lanes read the surfaces the first pass skipped (each with its own skeptic), and one agent deduped the whole register into work items with a single root cause each.  Corrections below **supersede Part I**, including headlines I wrote in `STATUS.md` and in the Slack closeout.

## A. Second-Round Verdicts On Every P1

All 40 Part I P1 findings were re-attacked by a skeptic instructed to disbelieve them and to require a concrete repro chain (file:line per step) before upholding.  Result: **UPHELD** 27, **UPHELD_NARROWED** 11, **ALREADY_FIXED** 2.  10 were downgraded below P1.  Money-path, accounting, backend and security batches ran on the frontier tier; the rest on the mid tier.

### Corrections That Change What Part I Said

#### **[api-contract-web-ios:api-03]** iOS Scan times out at 25s while the server scan budget is now 35s (#2830 today) — slow scans surface as "Check your connection"

- **Verdict** ALREADY_FIXED · confidence high
- **What Part I got wrong** The finding accurately describes a real defect that existed in the code snapshot it was written against (and STATUS.md's 2026-08-19 entries confirm testers on the currently-SHIPPED TestFlight binary 1.0.68 are still hitting it in the field, since that binary predates PR #2850). But on the branch as currently reviewed, the client-side constant is already fixed. This should be reclassified from a code defect to a deployment/rollout gap (old TF build vs new main).
- **What is actually true** ios/SocraticTrade/MobileAPIClient.swift:132-137 on the reviewed branch's current HEAD reads timeoutInterval = 50 with a comment stating the 35s server budget explicitly ('Client wait is longer so a late seed/200 is not dropped as a dead table'). `git show 1ca5073b7 -- ios/SocraticTrade/MobileAPIClient.swift` shows this was changed from 25->50 (comment from '20s' to '35s') as part of merged PR #2850. `git merge-base --is-ancestor 1ca5073b7 HEAD` confirms this commit is an ancestor of the reviewed branch's HEAD (5d453afef). app/api/scan/route.ts:22 INTERACTIVE_SCAN_BUDGET_MS=35_000 is unchanged, so the client now waits 50s -- 15s of slack beyond the server budget, exceeding even the finding's own recommended 45s fix.
- **Blast radius** None on the reviewed code. On the older shipped TF 1.0.68 binary still in testers' hands, the blast radius remains as described: a legitimately slow (25-35s) scan aborts client-side and shows a generic network error instead of results -- a UX annoyance, not a money-safety issue (no order is placed from a timed-out scan).

#### **[ux-copy-editor:copy-04]** iOS tells the user to edit the universe "on the Strategy page" — the app has no Strategy screen and on the web the universe edito…

- **Verdict** ALREADY_FIXED · confidence high
- **What Part I got wrong** Fully fixed by PR #2857, commit 437f0a4e4 ('iOS Guardrails names + fold #2849 Desk subtitle'), which is an ANCESTOR of this branch's tip (git log: 5d453afef docs-review commit is a child of 437f0a4e4). Verified current ios/SocraticTrade/HomeView.swift:94/109 no longer says 'Strategy page' — the checklist detail now reads `DeskCopy.universeNeedsIndex` = 'Choose at least one base index (e.g. S&P 500) or add extra symbols so the strategy has names to scan.' and the follow-up hint reads `DeskCopy.universeRefreshAfterGuardrails` = 'Add an index or extra symbols on Guardrails, then pull to refresh here.' (DeskModels.swift:668-673). Verified GuardrailsView.swift no longer contains 'Universe edits stay on the Strategy page' (grep for 'Strategy page' across ios/ and app/ returns only two Swift TEST assertions that it's ABSENT: ios/SocraticTradeTests/DeskModelsTests.swift:283 `XCTAssertFalse(Desk…
- **What is actually true** N/A — fixed on this branch's own history before the review-docs commit was added.
- **Blast radius** None currently reachable — the copy now correctly names Guardrails as the destination, matching the real web location at app/console/guardrails/page.tsx:425, and two regression tests pin the fix against recurrence.

#### **[backend-reliability:berel-02]** listFillEvents returns the OLDEST 500 fills (ORDER BY filled_at ASC LIMIT 500) so P&L, open lots, tax, model-stats and dashboard…

- **Verdict** UPHELD_NARROWED → **P2** · confidence high
- **What Part I got wrong** Two claims do not hold up. (a) 'A 15-minute-cadence account reaches 500 fills in weeks' — the shipped cadence is defaults.ts:165 runCadenceMinutes: 60 with defaults.ts:138 maxProposalsPerRun: 3 and defaults.ts:137 maxDailyOrders: 10, so the ceiling is 10 fills/day and the floor for 500 is ~50 trading days on ONE book; there is no 15-minute cadence in the defaults. Whether any prod (account, source) has actually crossed 500 is not answerable from this tree — both local DBs have 0 fill_events rows. (b) The blast list is too wide: it does not reach the order path. Daily order/notional caps read trade_proposals unbounded (db-execution.ts:58-62), the PDT gate reads fill_events date-bounded with no LIMIT (db-execution.ts:155), and pending-reconciliation reads have no LIMIT (db-fills.ts:356-372).
- **What is actually true** Same defect as perf-01, cited correctly. src/lib/db-fills.ts:332 `ORDER BY filled_at ASC LIMIT ?` returns the OLDEST N; I confirmed all 16 non-test call sites pass 500 except strategy-tuning.ts:418 which passes 30 (rg over src/ and app/: robinhood-pnl-crosscheck.ts:60, tax.ts:337, experience-memory.ts:263 and 350 (5000), performance.ts:460/461/468/492/493, dashboard.ts:675/676, outcome-engine.ts:964, strategy-tuning.ts:335/409/410/417/418, strategy.ts:824/825, app/api/llm-usage/model-stats/route.ts:58). The aggravation this finding names is the part I can prove bites on current data: strategy-tuning.ts:415-419 picks `listFillEvents(accountNumber, source, 30, userId)` (30 OLDEST) and strategy-tuning.ts:603 ships `fills.slice(0, 20)` to the model as `recentFills`; strategy-tuning.ts:420-421 + performance.ts:1354 gate the whole weight-shift path behind >=20 closed lots (>=40 fills), so the…
- **Blast radius** Identical to perf-01 (same query). Proven-live portion: the paid strategy-tuning LLM review argues from the 20 oldest fills labelled 'recentFills' (strategy-tuning.ts:603) and derives `currentRegimeFromLots` from a truncated lot replay (strategy-tuning.ts:334-342), so weight nudges that steer which names get proposed are grounded in the account's first trades. #### **[qa-test-strategy:qa-05]** No global network guard in vitest; suite still reaches SEC/Yahoo/SiliconFlow

- **Verdict** UPHELD_NARROWED → **P2** · confidence high
- **What Part I got wrong** Downgraded P1 -> P2: verified both named live-network sinks are read-only informational calls — SiliconFlow/OpenRouter embeddings (src/lib/vector-db.ts:2440,2611) and SEC/Yahoo market-data fetchers — with no order-placement or fund-movement code path reachable from a test process. The underlying claim (no global fetch guard exists; CI hits live third-party hosts) is fully correct, but the worst realistic outcome is CI flakiness and small wasted third-party API quota, not corrupted trading data, a wrong order, or financial loss — a materially lower blast radius than qa-01 through qa-04, which touch order/proposal/ship-pipeline correctness on a real account.
- **What is actually true** 1) vitest.config.ts has no `setupFiles` entry at all — only `globalSetup: './test/global-setup.ts'` (confirmed by reading the full file), which is purely per-run TMPDIR/TMP/TEMP plumbing for temp SQLite DBs, not a network guard (confirmed by reading global-setup.ts in full). 2) Any test file that reaches src/lib/vector-db.ts (embeddings/rerank against https://api.siliconflow.cn or https://openrouter.ai, lines 2440/2611) or a market-data fetcher without a per-file mock makes a real outbound HTTP call. 3) STATUS.md:114 (current, from the 2026-08-18 RAG rollout) confirms verbatim: 'Full npm test still hits leftover SiliconFlow + SEC/Yahoo 404s (untouched).' 4) This produces nondeterministic timeouts/429s under CI's ubuntu-latest runner, consistent with maxWorkers being pinned to 1.
- **Blast radius** CI reliability/cost only: intermittent test failures and unnecessary calls to SiliconFlow/SEC/Yahoo endpoints during every `npm test` run; no money-path or user-facing-data-integrity impact is reachable from this gap.

## B. Gap Coverage — Five Surfaces Part I Only Skimmed

50 new findings across five lanes, each re-checked by its own skeptic (**UPHELD** 45, **UPHELD_NARROWED** 5).  These are additive to Part I's register.

### Console pages read in full (settings, guardrails, strategy, results, orders, activity, macro, decisions)

Server side, src/lib/db-profiles.ts:895-901 does `stripIdentityFields(stripUserFields(sourcePolicyRaw…
  - **Why** Every other place in the app that can flip an account from Ask-first to Autopilot (guardrails/page.tsx AutonomyCard) requires typing the literal word "AUTOPILOT", and every other place that loosens a guardrail on a live account requires typing CONFIRM (`needsTyped = reality.tone === "live" && hasLooser && policy.requireTypedConfirmation !== false`, used identically in policy-form.tsx:335 and strategy/page.tsx:1259 f…
  - **Fix** Route importAccountSettings through the same needsTyped/TypedConfirm gate used by PolicySaveBar and AiReviewPanel: diff the source policy against the target's current policy the same way reviewChanges() does, and require the CONFIRM phrase whenever the target is live and any changed field is a loosening (including strategyAuthority propose→decide). At minimum, explicitly preserve the target's own strategyAuthority t…

### Coach / assistant, end to end (including the trade-staging path)

- **[coach-01]** (P1 · bug · cross-surface · effort S) draft_order silently coerces any non-"sell" side value (case-sensitive) to "buy", including "short"/"cover"/typos
  - **Where** `src/lib/chat/tools.ts:92` · **Evidence** const side = input.side === "sell" ? "sell" : "buy"; // line 92; line 94 has the same pattern: const order_type = input.order_type === "limit" ? "limit" : "market";
  - **Why** This is a strict, case-sensitive equality check with no rejection path. Any side value that is not the exact lowercase string "sell" -- "Sell", "SELL", "short", "cover", a typo, or an out-of-schema value a less-compliant provider emits -- is silently mapped to "buy", the OPPOSITE economic direction from a sell/short. The Coach model catalog includes Gemini, Grok, Mistral, DeepSeek, Moonshot, and any free-typed custo…
  - **Fix** Reject the draft (return {error:"INVALID_SIDE"}) when input.side is not exactly "buy" or "sell" (case-insensitive compare, then fail closed on anything else, mirroring create_alert's normalizeAlertOp pattern), and do the same for order_type. If short/cover intent is out of scope for chat, say so explicitly in the draft error rather than silently rewriting the order to a buy.
- **[coach-02]** (P2 · bug · cross-surface · effort M) Neither the chat "Cancel" button nor the 120s turn deadline actually aborts an in-flight LLM provider request
  - **Where** `src/lib/chat/llm.ts:333` · **Evidence** async function defaultTransport(body, apiKey) { const res = await llmFetch("https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages", { method: "POST", headers: {...}, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); ... } -- no `signal` is ever passed, so llm-request.ts:402 falls back to `signal: init.signal ?? AbortSignal.timeout(L…
  - **Why** AnthropicLLM.run/OpenAILLM.run only check `args.abortSignal?.aborted` (llm.ts:395, 537) and the deadline budget (via decideStageBudget) at the TOP of each tool-loop step, BEFORE calling `this.transport(...)`. The Transport type itself is `(body, apiKey) => Promise<any>` -- it never receives the AbortSignal, so once a provider HTTP call is in flight there is no way to interrupt it; it can only be interrupted by its o…
  - **Fix** Thread `args.abortSignal` into the Transport signature and pass it as `signal` on every llmFetch call in defaultTransport/defaultOpenAITransport/makeOpenAITransport (composed with the existing timeout signal via e.g. AbortSignal.any). Also give the client's `/api/chat` fetch its own AbortController tied to the Cancel button so cancel takes effect locally without waiting on the server.
- **[coach-04]** (P2 · risk · backend · effort M) Chat conversation history has no account scoping -- the last-10-turn context replayed into every prompt spans ALL of a user's connected accounts
  - **Where** `src/lib/types.ts:3011` · **Evidence** export interface ChatTurn { id: string; userId: string; role: ChatTurnRole; text: string; citations: string[]; intent: string | null; redacted: boolean; model?: string | null; clientTurnId?: string | null; createdAt: string; } -- no connectedAccountId field anywhere on the row; orchestrator.ts:143…
  - **Why** A user with multiple connected accounts (e.g. a live account and a paper/practice account) who switches the active account mid-conversation gets a multi-turn LLM context that silently mixes both accounts' answers with no account-boundary marker in the replayed text -- the model has no way to know a prior turn's positions/P&L/portfolio numbers belonged to a different account than the one the current tool calls now re…
  - **Fix** Either scope chat_turns (and the 10-turn replay window) by connectedAccountId so switching accounts starts a fresh context, or inject an explicit account-boundary marker into the replayed history whenever the active account differs from the account each historical turn was answered under (would require persisting connectedAccountId per turn either way).
- **[coach-05]** (P2 · risk · api · effort S) Dead, unauthenticated POST /api/chat-history lets a user forge arbitrary chat turns -- including fabricated "assistant" replies -- into their own persisted transcript

- **[coach-06]** (P2 · risk · backend · effort M) Coach URL-lesson capture stores fetched web-page text verbatim (no summarization despite the name) as auto-approved "fact"-tier learned context, protected only by a financial-risk keyword classifier, not a general injection detector

  - **Where** `src/lib/chat/llm.ts:436` · **Evidence** for (let step = 0; step < MAX_STEPS; step++) { ... if (resp.stop_reason !== "tool_use" || toolUses.length === 0) break; ... /* execute tools, push results, loop continues */ } -- MAX_STEPS = 5 (llm.ts:50). If step index 4 (the last allowed iteration) STILL returns tool_use, the tool results are exe…
  - **Why** `text` retains whatever the LAST model response contained -- typically empty or near-empty, since a response whose stop_reason is tool_use rarely carries prose alongside the tool call. The function then returns `withDisclaimer(text)` (Anthropic) or `text || DISCLAIMER` (OpenAI), i.e. the user's answer is silently reduced to just "This is general information, not personalized financial advice." with zero substantive…
  - **Fix** On exiting the loop specifically because the last step still wanted tools (rather than because the model naturally stopped), either allow one final no-tools completion call to summarize what was gathered so far, or set an explicit flag/text ("I gathered partial information but ran out of steps -- ask again for more detail") instead of falling through to a bare disclaimer.

### Watchlist + price alerts + notification delivery, end to end

- **[alerts-01]** (P1 · bug · backend · effort M) Price alerts silently stop evaluating when no broker account is active
  - **Where** `src/lib/alerts.ts:68` · **Evidence** const accountNumber = policy.accountNumber; if (!accountNumber) return [];
  - **Why** checkPriceAlerts() calls getPolicy(userId) with no connectedAccountId, which resolves to whichever connected account is currently 'active' for that user (src/lib/db-profiles.ts resolveAccount -> getActiveConnectedAccount). PriceAlert rows are user-scoped, not account-scoped (types.ts:127-138), and the watchlist page banner literally says 'yours across every account' -- yet evaluation is gated on one account's accoun…
  - **Fix** Evaluate price alerts against an account chosen explicitly for quote purposes (not the mutable 'active' pointer), or fall back to any workable connected account, and emit an explicit 'alerts paused: no account' audit/UI state when none exists at all.
- **[alerts-02]** (P1 · bug · backend · effort S) checkPriceAlerts swallows broker-quote errors with zero logging
  - **Where** `src/lib/alerts.ts:74` · **Evidence** let quotes: Record<string, { price?: number }> = {}; try { quotes = await gateway.getEquityQuotes(accountNumber, symbols); } catch { return []; }
  - **Why** Any exception from the broker gateway (auth failure, rate limit, network outage) is caught and discarded with an empty catch block -- no console.error, no audit() call, nothing recorded anywhere. checkAllUserPriceAlerts()'s own .catch() (alerts.ts:120) never fires because checkPriceAlerts() resolves normally with []. A persistent broker problem on one user's active account means every armed alert is silently re-chec…
  - **Fix** At minimum audit()/console.error the caught exception, mirroring how checkAllUserPriceAlerts already logs per-user errors, so a sustained broker failure is discoverable instead of indistinguishable from 'nothing to check.'
  - **Narrowed by verifier** The finding's framing ('any exception from the broker gateway') slightly overstates the practical blast radius: Alpaca/Tradier/Robinhood's own getEquityQuotes implementations already self-heal internally (try/catch + Yahoo-daily-close fallback in alpaca.ts:562-579 and tradier.ts:620-644; per-chunk…
- **[alerts-03]** (P2 · bug · cross-surface · effort M) Price-alert push deep link's ?symbol= is dropped by both web and iOS
  - **Where** `src/lib/push-deep-links.ts:99` · **Evidence** case "price_alert": return routing.symbol ? `/console/watchlist?symbol=${encodeURIComponent(routing.symbol)}` : "/console/watchlist";
  - **Why** The server builds a symbol-specific URL and the file's header comment calls the query param 'additive' routing for 'a client that prefers structured routing.' Neither client honors it: app/console/watchlist/page.tsx never calls useSearchParams or reads the URL, so tapping a 'Price alert: NVDA' notification on the web lands on the full unfiltered watchlist/alerts list. ios/SocraticTrade/DeepLink.swift:75-77 matches t…
  - **Fix** Implement symbol-aware routing on both the watchlist page (scroll-to/highlight via useSearchParams) and DeepLink.swift (a `.watchlist(symbol:)` destination mirroring `.proposal(id:)`), or stop implying finer-grained routing exists in the push-deep-links.ts comment.
  - Sharpens `ios-engineering:ios-16`
- **[alerts-04]** (P2 · bug · ios · effort S) iOS watchlist/alert commands queue behind a 30-minute strategy run
  - **Where** `src/lib/mobile-api.ts:88` · **Evidence** const IMMEDIATE_MOBILE_COMMAND_TYPES = new Set<MobileCommandType>([ ...IMMEDIATE_PROTECTIVE_COMMAND_TYPES, "account.activate", "order.cancel" ]);
  - **Why** watchlist.add, watchlist.remove, alert.create, and alert.delete are not in IMMEDIATE_MOBILE_COMMAND_TYPES, so from iOS they go through the single, strictly-serial mobile_commands worker (claimNextQueuedCommand picks the globally oldest queued row one at a time, mobile-api.ts:701-717) alongside strategy.run_once, which the same file documents as legitimately taking up to STALE_MOBILE_COMMAND_THRESHOLD_MS = 30 minutes…
  - **Fix** Add watchlist.add/remove and alert.create/delete to IMMEDIATE_MOBILE_COMMAND_TYPES -- none of them touch the broker or need serialization with a strategy run.
  - Sharpens `api-contract-web-ios:api-05`
- **[alerts-05]** (P2 · risk · backend · effort M) checkPriceAlerts/checkAllUserPriceAlerts have zero test coverage
  - **Where** `test/watchlist-alerts.test.ts:1` · **Evidence** describe("price alerts", () => { it("normalizes comparison operators"...); it("creates, lists, and deletes alerts"...); it("rejects invalid alert input"...); }) -- grep across test/ for checkPriceAlerts|checkAllUserPriceAlerts returns zero hits anywhere in the repo.
  - **Why** The entire money-relevant behavior of this feature -- does a crossing get detected, does a missing/invalid quote get skipped instead of false-triggering, does a broker error leave the alert armed for a later retry, does a trigger call sendNotification exactly once -- lives in checkPriceAlerts/checkAllUserPriceAlerts (src/lib/alerts.ts) and is completely untested. Only CRUD (create/list/delete) and input validation a…
  - **Fix** Add unit tests covering: a crossing that should fire, a quote below/above threshold that should not, a missing/zero quote (skip), a broker-gateway throw (should not crash and should leave the alert armed), and no-active-account (should return [] without calling the gateway), using an injected fake gateway/policy the way other broker-dependent tests in this repo already do.
- **[alerts-06]** (P2 · risk · backend · effort M) Price alerts fire against raw quotes with no staleness check
  - **Where** `src/lib/alerts.ts:83` · **Evidence** const currentPrice = quote?.price; if (typeof currentPrice !== "number" || currentPrice <= 0) continue; const hit = alert.op === "<" ? currentPrice < alert.price : currentPrice > alert.price;
  - **Why** The order-placement path has a dedicated staleness gate (quoteAgeSecForStalenessGate in src/lib/quotes-cascade.ts, applied in policy.ts:395-475) that checks quote age before acting on a price. checkPriceAlerts uses none of that infrastructure -- it takes whatever `.price` the broker gateway returns with no age check at all. A delayed/cached quote (a provider outage falling back to a stale cached value, or a venue-de…
  - **Fix** Reuse quoteAgeSecForStalenessGate (or the quote's own asOf/timestamp) to skip evaluating an alert against a quote older than a small threshold, and/or annotate the triggered notification with quote age so a delayed trigger is honestly labeled.
- **[alerts-07]** (P2 · bug · backend · effort M) An alert is marked 'triggered' before delivery is attempted, with no retry if every channel fails
  - **Where** `src/lib/alerts.ts:88` · **Evidence** const updated = markPriceAlertTriggered(alert.id, userId, currentPrice); if (!updated) continue; triggered.push(updated); audit("alert.triggered", {...}); await sendNotification(...)
  - **Why** markPriceAlertTriggered flips the alert's DB status to 'triggered' (one-shot, non-reversible per src/lib/db-api-keys.ts:1777-1781) before sendNotification is even awaited. Since price alerts never re-arm by design, if every enabled delivery channel fails at send time (a revoked Pushover token, a wrong ntfy topic, an expired APNs cert, or simply zero channels configured yet), sendNotification records a status:'failed…
  - **Fix** Either don't flip the alert to 'triggered' until sendNotification reports at least one successful channel (retrying next tick if delivery fully failed), or surface undelivered-but-triggered alerts more prominently on the Watchlist page instead of blending in with delivered ones.
- **[alerts-08]** (P3 · improvement · cross-surface · effort M) Price alerts can only be created or deleted -- no edit, pause, or snooze
  - **Where** `app/api/alerts/route.ts:1` · **Evidence** export async function GET... export async function POST... export async function DELETE... -- no PUT/PATCH handler; src/lib/alerts.ts exports only createAlert/listAlerts/removeAlert, no updateAlert.

The review brief specifically asks about edit/mute/snooze lifecycle actions and none exist for price alerts -- the Alert Center's 24h mute is display-only, for a different set of event types, and doesn't apply to price_alert at all.
  - **Fix** Add a PUT/PATCH /api/alerts endpoint (and a matching mobile-api.ts alert.update command) for price/op/note edits, and consider a simple armed/paused toggle for snoozing without deleting.

  - **Where** `src/lib/alerts.ts:17` · **Evidence** const SYMBOL_RE = /^[A-Z.]{1,10}$/; (alerts.ts) vs return /^[A-Z][A-Z0-9.]{0,9}$/.test(normalized) && !normalized.includes("..") && !normalized.endsWith("."); (index-universes.ts:311, isValidAppSymbol)

PriceAlert rows are user-scoped, not account-scoped (types.ts:127-138), so this tag is incidental, not meaningful. app/console/activity/page.tsx:213 filters the Activity feed to the currently active account, so a price-al…

### First-run and account lifecycle (connect → activate → run; sign-out, expiry, deletion)

- **[lifecycle-01]** (P1 · bug · cross-surface · effort S) A disconnected ('draining') account can be reactivated and becomes the live trading target while the scheduler is still tearing it down
  - **Where** `src/lib/db-api-keys.ts:1323` · **Evidence** setActiveConnectedAccount(id, userId) only checks the row exists for this user, then flips is_active — it never reads or clears is_draining: `db.prepare("UPDATE connected_accounts SET is_active = 0 WHERE user_id = ?").run(userId); db.prepare("UPDATE connected_accounts SET is_active = 1 WHERE id = ?…
  - **Why** A user who clicks Disconnect then immediately re-Loads/re-Uses the same account (e.g. changed their mind, or it's still showing in the list because purge hasn't run yet) makes that account the full policy target (getPolicy -> resolveAccount -> getActiveConnectedAccount, which also has no isDraining filter) while the scheduler's drain routine is independently cancelling its open orders and racing to delete the row's…
  - **Fix** Have setActiveConnectedAccount reject (or auto-clear is_draining and cancel the pending purge) for a draining row; disable Load/Use and grey the Switch/Use rows client-side wherever isDraining is true (brokers.tsx, chrome.tsx ScopeSelector, HomeView.swift) instead of only checking isActive.
- **[lifecycle-02]** (P1 · defect · desktop-web · effort S) Settings > Broker connections mislabels every non-loaded account 'Inactive' even when it is actively trading in the background
  - **Where** `app/console/settings/brokers.tsx:189` · **Evidence** `const stateInfo = isCurrentLoaded && snapshot?.policy ? deriveStateInfo(snapshot.policy) : null;` (isCurrentLoaded === account.isActive). The chip render at lines 271-280 falls back to `<Chip tone="muted">{account.isActive ? "Loaded" : "Inactive"}</Chip>` whenever stateInfo is null -- which is eve…
  - **Why** A second connected account (e.g. a live Roth IRA) that is genuinely running Autopilot right now shows the same static 'Inactive' chip as an account that has never been armed. For a real-money multi-account trading app this is a materially misleading state readout on the exact screen (Settings > Broker connections) a user checks to answer 'is anything happening in my other account.'
  - **Fix** Compute stateInfo from `snapshot.connectedAccountPolicies?.[account.id]` for every row (as ScopeSelector already does), not only for the currently-loaded one.

But AccountSettingsView's accountsSection (lines 818-848) only ever renders ConnectedAccountSettingsRow for EXISTING accounts (activate-only, via 'Use') -- there is no Connect Alpaca/Robinhood/…
  - **Why** A first-time iOS-only user (downloaded the app without ever visiting the website) hits a genuine dead end: the checklist's own copy claims the fix is inside the very screen it links to, and only the empty-state variant of that same screen tells the truth.

- **[lifecycle-04]** (P2 · defect · desktop-web · effort S) Website session expiry inside an already-open console tab has no recovery path -- stuck behind a stale 'Unauthorized' banner, Run Once fails with a misleading generic message
  - **Where** `app/console/lib/useConsoleData.tsx:139` · **Evidence** middleware.ts:412-419 answers an unauthenticated `/api/*` request with a plain 401 (`new NextResponse("Unauthorized", { status: 401 })`), never a redirect (redirects only fire for page navigation, not fetch()). useConsoleData's runFetch (lines 139-150) treats that identically to any other transient…
  - **Why** A session that expires (or is invalidated) while the console tab stays open never tells the user to sign back in -- it just quietly degrades to stale data forever (polling every 15s, 401 every time) and, if they try to act, sends them to Activity to investigate 'why the run failed' instead of back to /login.
  - **Fix** Have the console's fetch client special-case 401 (distinct from other ConsoleApiErrors): stop the poll loop and route to /login (or show an explicit 'sign in again' banner) instead of the generic staleness/failure paths.
