## C. Deduped Work Plan — 45 Clusters

Every finding in the register (Part I + Part II, minus what the second round refuted or found already fixed) folded into work items that share ONE root cause, each with the single change that closes the whole cluster.  This is the list to work from — not the raw finding count.

### Tranche 1 — Money Correctness, Wrong Numbers, Security, Silent Stalls (22)

#### `account-write-guards` — Account-scoped write paths have no guards: one click can arm Autopilot or reactivate a draining account
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort M** · 4 findings: `pages-01`, `lifecycle-01`, `lifecycle-07`, `lifecycle-11`
- **Root cause** importAccountSettings (src/lib/db-profiles.ts:895) strips only identity and user-level fields, so strategyAuthority copies through and a single "Import settings" click can flip a live account from Ask-first to Autopilot with none of the TypedConfirm ceremony app/console/guardrails/page.tsx:700 requires; applyProfileToAccount (db-profiles.ts:731) and activateStrategyProfile (:685) do not strip it at all. setActiveConnectedAccount (src/lib/db-api-keys.ts:1323) never reads is_draining, and nothing ever clears the flag that deleteConnectedAccount sets at :1381, so a disconnected account can be re-loaded as the live target while the scheduler drains and purges it.
- **The one change** Treat strategyAuthority and is_draining as account-owned state in src/lib/db-profiles.ts:685/731/895 and src/lib/db-api-keys.ts:1323 — preserve authority the way systemState already is, refuse or clear draining on activate and on re-upsert — and route any authority change through the existing typed-confirm gate.

#### `broker-io-deadlines` — Broker I/O is unbounded in time and scope, and the protective scheduler lanes latch on it
- **P1 · backend · effort M** · 6 findings: `backend-reliability:berel-03`, `backend-reliability:berel-08`, `backend-reliability:berel-09`, `trading-money-path:tsx-05`, `frontend-performance:dweb-02`, `backend-reliability:berel-15`
- **Root cause** The Alpaca SDK is constructed with no timeout (src/lib/alpaca.ts:247; axios default 0), so getLatestQuotes/createOrder/cancelOrder can hang indefinitely — and the stop-monitor and stale-exit lanes clear their in-flight key only in .finally (src/lib/scheduler.ts:919/931) with no withDeadline, so one hung socket latches the lane and that account's synthetic stops stop being evaluated forever. The tick itself has no re-entrancy guard (scheduler.ts:411). Separately getEquityOrders asks for status:"all", limit 500 with pagination (alpaca.ts:502/508-518) on ~20 call sites including a 15s dashboard poll and two 60s scheduler lanes, and the trade-updates stream has no idle watchdog (src/lib/streams…
- **The one change** Pass a deadline into every Alpaca/Tradier call and wrap each scheduler lane in withDeadline the way src/lib/safety-maintenance.ts:69-78 already does, and scope getEquityOrders (src/lib/alpaca.ts:501) to open orders plus a bounded date window.

#### `cash-flow-and-trading-day` — Day P&L reads $0.00 and shorts mint phantom deposits: flows are inferred, not read, and the day is not the trading day
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort L** · 8 findings: `performance-accounting:perf-02`, `performance-accounting:perf-03`, `performance-accounting:perf-08`, `performance-accounting:perf-14`, `performance-accounting:perf-16`, `performance-accounting:perf-22`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-13`, `trading-money-path:tsx-17`
- **Root cause** app/console/lib/derive.ts:443 calls inferExternalCashFlows([baseline, fakeCurrent], []) with a hardcoded empty fill list, so on any day the strategy trades directionally tradeCash is 0 (src/lib/cash-flows.ts:157-164), the guard at :187-208 sets flow = deltaEquity, and Home prints exactly $0.00 (0.00%). src/lib/cash-flows.ts:164 also gives short/cover the wrong cash sign, minting a 2x-notional phantom deposit per short. Day keys are UTC (cash-flows.ts:33/129-142) while owner timestamps are Central, the risk-breaker HWM is UTC and not flow-adjusted (src/lib/risk-breaker.ts:86) and daily caps roll at NY midnight (src/lib/db-execution.ts:15). The broker's own equity/cash-activity ledger is impl…
- **The one change** Make the broker cash-activity ledger the flow source — wire src/lib/alpaca-account-insights.ts:167 into the dashboard and benchmark paths — and keep inference only as a labeled fallback with the short/cover sign, the real fill list and one shared Central trading-day helper reused by risk-breaker.ts:86 and db-execution.ts:15.

#### `coach-tools-and-turns` — Coach coerces invalid tool inputs instead of rejecting them, and cannot be cancelled mid-call
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort M** · 8 findings: `coach-01`, `coach-02`, `coach-07`, `coach-08`, `coach-09`, `coach-10`, `coach-11`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-08`
- **Root cause** src/lib/chat/tools.ts:92 maps any side that is not exactly "sell" — "Sell", "short", a typo — to "buy", the opposite economic direction, and :94/96 silently drops a limit price the same way; downstream promote-draft only re-checks that side is buy|sell, which is trivially true after coercion. qty carries no unit and there is no notional field (tools.ts:58), and k has no maximum (tools.ts:155) unlike its clamped siblings. Separately no transport receives args.abortSignal (src/lib/chat/llm.ts:332-346, :688-705), so Cancel and the 120s deadline cannot stop an in-flight provider call, and exhausting the 5-step loop returns only the disclaimer (llm.ts:436-459).
- **The one change** Fail closed on out-of-enum tool inputs and clamp/convert numerics server-side in src/lib/chat/tools.ts (mirroring normalizeAlertOp's fail-closed shape), and thread args.abortSignal into every transport in src/lib/chat/llm.ts so cancel and the turn deadline actually abort.

#### `console-ships-too-much` — The console ships server DB internals to the browser and the whole universe on every 15s poll
- **P1 · desktop-web · effort M** · 7 findings: `frontend-performance:dweb-01`, `frontend-performance:dweb-03`, `frontend-performance:dweb-04`, `frontend-performance:dweb-05`, `frontend-performance:dweb-15`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-04`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-15`
- **Root cause** app/console/settings/brokers.tsx:12 value-imports @/lib/venue-contract, which pulls source-settings → db-api-keys → db into the client bundle: SQL schema, migrations and key-store logic reach the browser, and this exact chain produced the 2026-08-18 'process.uptime is not a function' production crash (fixed symptomatically in 01f4e73e2, whose own comment names the chain). No module in the repo imports the server-only guard. On the payload side src/lib/dashboard.ts:1108 serializes raw audit[], the full 500-symbol quotesBySymbol and the entire order history into a 15s poll (app/console/lib/useConsoleData.tsx:23), and post-action refreshes are served from the 10s cache so acked alerts stay on…
- **The one change** Add server-only to the src/lib/db* modules and split the pure mergeAccountCapabilities helper out of venue-contract so brokers.tsx no longer drags the DB layer, then project the snapshot at src/lib/dashboard.ts:1108 down to the fields the UI actually reads and bust the cache on mutation.

#### `event-loop-pins` — RAG mirroring pins the single serving+trading event loop for minutes at a time
- **P1 · backend · effort L** · 7 findings: `backend-reliability:berel-01`, `backend-reliability:berel-04`, `backend-reliability:berel-06`, `backend-reliability:berel-07`, `backend-reliability:berel-10`, `backend-reliability:berel-11`, `backend-reliability:berel-12`
- **Root cause** src/lib/db-learning.ts:1613-1616 makes idempotency a DELETE with equality on non-rowid FTS5 columns, which FTS5 serves as a full-corpus scan — measured 4.3ms/row at 10k rows rising to 26.3ms/row at 60k, and ~300ms/chunk at the live corpus per the repo's own receipt in src/lib/rag/fts-mirror-bound.ts:20-23, while the INSERT it was blamed on costs 0.1-0.4ms. src/lib/rag/persist-local-complete.ts:46-54 and src/lib/web-sources/sec-filings.ts:624-631/736-756 then loop that call over a whole filing with no yield, no transaction, and no hasInFlightStrategyWork() gate (scheduler.ts:589-602) — unlike the bounded SEC worker at sec-ingest-worker.ts:462-475. There is no event-loop lag instrumentation a…
- **The one change** Give the FTS mirror a rowid/side-key so idempotency becomes an indexed lookup instead of a scan (src/lib/db-learning.ts:1613 plus the DDL at src/lib/db.ts:2148), and route every mirror caller through the existing planFtsMirrorSlice + yieldEventLoop + strategy gate the SEC worker already uses.

#### `green-request-schema` — Bull strict JSON schema is invalid, so every OpenAI-family Green seat 400s before generation
- **P1 · backend · effort S** · 2 findings: `llm-agent-architecture:llm-01`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-08`
- **Root cause** src/lib/strategy.ts:5870 declares exitPlan inside the Bull item's properties while BULL_PROPOSAL_REQUIRED_KEYS (strategy.ts:6653, used at :5851 for the required array) omits it, under additionalProperties:false — OpenAI strict mode requires required ⊇ properties, so any native-OpenAI or openai/* routed seat is rejected at the API layer 100% of the time (the fast 400 misdiagnosed as 'terra unservable' in #2831). The same file then trusts provider schema enforcement on json_object transports (strategy.ts:6115) where nothing enforces it.
- **The one change** Derive required from properties at src/lib/strategy.ts:5851 (or add exitPlan to BULL_PROPOSAL_REQUIRED_KEYS at :6653), add the invariant test that required ⊇ properties, and add a post-parse validator for the json_object transports.

#### `home-proposal-rows` — Home's latest-run rows use synthetic ids and a private status map: green chips on failures, an Approve button that 404s, no keyboard access
- **P1 · desktop-web · effort M** · 3 findings: `desktop-web-ux:dweb-01`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-18`, `web-accessibility:a11y-01`
- **Root cause** app/console/page.tsx:79 composes the row id as `${runId}-${symbol}-${status}` while real proposal ids are crypto.randomUUID (src/lib/strategy.ts:3361), so handleApprove always 404s at app/api/proposals/[id]/approve/route.ts:26 — and page.tsx:1011 offers that button on 'blocked' rows, a routine strategy outcome. page.tsx:1025's local chipTone knows only 'blocked'/'failed'/'pending', so the real statuses 'error' and 'proposed' fall through to green while labels.ts renders the word "Failed". The row itself is a click-only div (page.tsx:1029-1030) with no role, tabIndex or key handler, so the details sheet and its Approve flow are unreachable by keyboard.
- **The one change** Render those rows from the persisted proposals (real ids) through the shared helpers in app/console/lib/action-verbs.ts / labels.ts, and make the row a keyboard-operable control with the nested SymbolButton excluded from the activation target.

#### `identity-fails-open` — Identity resolution falls back to the owner instead of failing closed
- **P1 · backend · effort M** · 3 findings: `security-auth:sec-01`, `security-auth:sec-07`, `security-auth:sec-08`
- **Root cause** middleware.ts:385-388 sets trustedEmail = PRIMARY_EMAIL whenever isAuthConfigured() is false, and nothing asserts AUTH_SECRET in production — instrumentation.ts:47 asserts only ENCRYPTION_KEY — so a selective env regression makes every anonymous request the owner, with x-authenticated-user-email stamped at middleware.ts:420 and no route outside src/lib/auth/admin.ts checking identity provenance. The same shape repeats at src/lib/request-user.ts:28-34 (missing header → DEV_USER_ID 'local') and across 228 db functions that default userId="local" (src/lib/db-learning.ts:12); middleware.ts:435's matcher additionally skips any path ending in a static extension.
- **The one change** Assert AUTH_SECRET at boot when DB_BOOTSTRAP=live alongside the existing ENCRYPTION_KEY assert (instrumentation.ts:47), gate the middleware.ts:385 local fallback on that same production marker (the dev branch is pinned by ~20 tests, so add rather than remove), and drop the userId="local" defaults so a forgotten argument is a type error.

#### `ios-state-outcome-truth` — iOS shows the previous account's data after sign-out and never surfaces the outcome of a money action
- **P1 · ios · effort M** · 8 findings: `ios-engineering:ios-01`, `ios-engineering:ios-04`, `ios-engineering:ios-07`, `ios-engineering:ios-14`, `ios-engineering:ios-15`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-01`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-11`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-12`
- **Root cause** clearLocalSession (ios/SocraticTrade/MobileStore.swift:628-650) nils in-memory state but never removes the UserDefaults blob at :136-144, so a cold launch after sign-out or account deletion re-reads it, sets isAuthenticated from disk, and offline renders the prior account's holdings as current; init also stamps that cached blob lastUpdatedAt = Date() so it shows "Updated · now". On the write side, store.error has exactly one renderer (SnapshotScaffold, AppComponents.swift:566) which sits at scroll-offset 0 behind the settings sheet and is cleared by load() at MobileStore.swift:292, so a failed guardrail edit is invisible; 401 handling differs per passthrough (:463) and success has no acknow…
- **The one change** Make one cache/session owner on the Swift side — clear the UserDefaults snapshot (and move the cookie to the Keychain) inside clearLocalSession, persist the real capture time, and render store.error and success as an .alert/toast anchored to the presenting view instead of a card at scroll-top that load() wipes.

#### `market-cache-freshness` — Calendar-day freshness math freezes screener, OHLC and enrichment caches during live sessions
- **P1 · backend · effort M** · 7 findings: `market-data-integrity:mdi-01`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-02`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-08`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-10`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-11`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-12`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-15`
- **Root cause** Freshness is measured in calendar days, not trading sessions. src/lib/market-hours.ts:283-311 isWeekendOrHolidayClosureAhead returns true for the whole of Friday (and the whole day before any holiday), so expiresAtRespectingMarketClose pushes a 5-minute screener TTL to Monday's open — verified: a Fri 10:00 ET write expires Mon 09:30 ET. src/lib/history.ts:115-126 isBarSeriesFresh accepts a 3-calendar-day-old last bar, so a Monday write suppresses any refetch through Tuesday and Wednesday. The same module has no early-close calendar (market-hours.ts:35), and the universe/history cascades below it mix adjustment bases and a 2026-06-14 static index snapshot.
- **The one change** Replace the calendar-day helpers with a session-aware pair — extend TTL only after the session actually closes (src/lib/market-hours.ts:288/305) and measure bar freshness in elapsed sessions (src/lib/history.ts:115) — and add the half-day/early-close table the same module needs.

#### `order-provenance-guard` — Automation cancel-replaces orders it does not own (bracket take-profit legs, owner GTC sells, manually cancelled stops)
- **P1 · backend · effort M** · 4 findings: `trading-money-path:tsx-02`, `trading-money-path:tsx-14`, `trading-money-path:tsx-08`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-12`
- **Root cause** No enqueue or pump path checks who placed the order. src/lib/order-replacement.ts:754-757 filters only on side and state==="held", so a just-activated bracket take-profit leg (state "new", 15 min after entry fill) passes; the pump re-checks only type/held/quantity at :333-364 and never reads EquityOrder.orderClass (src/lib/types.ts:939, mapped at src/lib/alpaca.ts:942); it then cancels and places a MARKET order at :452-477. The protective-stop reconciler (src/lib/order-cancel.ts:198) re-places a stop the owner just cancelled, contradicting the cancel sheet's "No new order is placed."
- **The one change** Add an order-provenance check (orderClass/legs + app-placed clientOrderId + a manual-cancel tombstone) at src/lib/order-replacement.ts:754 and src/lib/order-cancel.ts:198 so bracket legs and owner-placed/owner-cancelled orders are never auto-replaced, and drop the per-tick deferral spam plus the misleading alert at order-replacement.ts:760-767.

#### `per-account-visibility` — Multi-account screens show the active account's data labeled as every account's
- **P1 · desktop-web · effort M** · 5 findings: `lifecycle-02`, `lifecycle-12`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-12`, `pages-04`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-10`
- **Root cause** app/console/settings/brokers.tsx:189 computes stateInfo only when isCurrentLoaded, so every other connected account chips a static "Inactive" even while it is genuinely running Autopilot (the scheduler runs each account independently, src/lib/scheduler.ts:810); brokers.tsx:191 then filters snapshot.pendingProposals, which src/lib/dashboard.ts:690 already scoped to the active account, so the per-account pending count is structurally always zero. app/console/decisions/page.tsx:28 fetches /api/socratic/decisions with no connectedAccountId although the route supports it, interleaving both accounts' BUY/SELL traces with no per-row label.
- **The one change** Feed every per-account row from snapshot.connectedAccountPolicies (already built at src/lib/dashboard.ts:350-368 and already used by chrome.tsx:141) plus a real per-account pending count, and scope /console/decisions by connectedAccountId with a visible account chip per row.

#### `placement-outcome-truth` — Approve reports success when nothing was placed, and misfiles retryable broker errors as terminal
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort L** · 6 findings: `api-contract-web-ios:api-02`, `trading-money-path:tsx-03`, `trading-money-path:tsx-15`, `trading-money-path:tsx-12`, `trading-money-path:tsx-04`, `trading-money-path:tsx-01`
- **Root cause** executeProposal returns a status object instead of throwing for every non-placement outcome (src/lib/strategy-execution.ts:239-246 declares Promise<{status:string}>; busy at :298, blocked at :498/766/1113/1136/1251/1302, not_placed at :1338/1394, error at :1319-1473) and every consumer treats "resolved" as "placed": src/lib/mobile-api.ts:1080 stamps finishCommand(command,"succeeded",result), the Swift MobileCommand has no result field (ios/SocraticTrade/MobileModels.swift:511-520) so ProposalsView.swift:481 paints a green "Approved", and the same resolver files any HTTP 4xx — 429 and 408 included — as terminal rejected_by_broker at src/lib/strategy-execution.ts:1279.
- **The one change** Introduce one PlacementOutcome resolver at src/lib/strategy-execution.ts:1279/:239 that classifies {placed, blocked, busy, retryable, rejected}; return it verbatim with an honest command status at src/lib/mobile-api.ts:1080, decode result.status/reasons in ios/SocraticTrade/MobileModels.swift:511, and render it on both clients the way app/console/components/approval-card.tsx:347-366 already does.

#### `price-alert-evaluation` — Price alerts stop evaluating silently — no active account, one broker, swallowed errors, no age check
- **P1 · backend · effort M** · 5 findings: `alerts-01`, `alerts-02`, `alerts-05`, `alerts-06`, `alerts-09`
- **Root cause** src/lib/alerts.ts:67-69 returns [] whenever the console-active account has no accountNumber (zero connected accounts, or the active one was deleted — deleteConnectedAccount at src/lib/db-api-keys.ts:1376 promotes nobody), even though PriceAlert rows are user-scoped; :73-78 catches any gateway throw in an empty catch with no log or audit, so a Public.com/eToro API error is indistinguishable from "nothing to check"; :83 acts on the price with no staleness check although quoteAgeSecForStalenessGate exists; and :17's SYMBOL_RE disagrees with isValidAppSymbol (src/lib/index-universes.ts:311), so malformed symbols sit armed forever. Zero tests cover the evaluation loop.
- **The one change** Switch checkPriceAlerts to the user-scoped fetchFreshQuotesCascade(symbols, userId) (src/lib/quotes-cascade.ts:149) instead of gateway.getEquityQuotes on the active account — this closes the no-account gate and the single-broker error in one edit — then log the caught error, apply quoteAgeSecForStalenessGate, share isValidAppSymbol, and add the evaluation tests.

#### `quote-value-provenance` — Quote pipeline fabricates asOf and picks the wrong field as price, defeating the staleness gate
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort L** · 9 findings: `market-data-integrity:mdi-03`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-04`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-05`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-06`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-07`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-09`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-16`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-17`, `market-data-integrity:mdi-18`
- **Root cause** Values that were not just observed are stamped with the wall clock: src/lib/data-providers.ts:1571-1590 takeScalar writes cascadeFetchedAt = new Date().toISOString() for any field lacking its own asOf, so a 6h-cached nasdaq-quote volume wins recency arbitration at src/lib/evidence-facts.ts:132-155 and overwrites base.volume; src/lib/market.ts:1548 (Yahoo quote-only) and src/lib/quotes-cascade.ts:397 (ROIC profile price) hardcode asOf: new Date(), so policy.ts:404-413's opening-order staleness gate always reads ~0s old; and src/lib/alpaca.ts:551-569 returns the ASK as price, which becomes the displayed price, watchlist price, alert trigger and stop mark.
- **The one change** Make asOf non-forgeable — carry the provider's own observation timestamp (or leave it undefined, which src/lib/quotes-cascade.ts:47-61 already treats as not-fresh) through takeScalar, toQuoteOnlyMarketQuote and the ROIC/CBOE/VWAP tiers, use mid/last instead of ask at alpaca.ts:551, and surface the resulting age in the drilldown, watchlist and scan chips.

#### `run-scoped-account` — Run-scoped code re-resolves the console-active account instead of the account being traded
- **P1 · backend · effort M** · 3 findings: `llm-agent-architecture:llm-02`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-05`, `coach-04`
- **Root cause** src/lib/red-team.ts:192 calls getActiveConnectedAccount(userId) and :203 calls getStrategyPrompt(userId) with no account argument, even though debateProposal was handed runPolicy carrying the correct connectedAccountId — so with two accounts scheduled concurrently the adversarial reviewer can veto on the wrong venue's short capability and reason from another account's custom strategy prompt (deriveExecutionState at :196 has the same bug). src/lib/learned-context/store.ts:401 appends approved directives to the active account's prompt regardless of the pending row's account, and chat turns carry no account at all (src/lib/types.ts:3011) so the 10-turn replay mixes accounts.
- **The one change** Thread the owning connectedAccountId explicitly through src/lib/red-team.ts:192/196/203, src/lib/learned-context/store.ts:401 and the chat_turns row plus its replay window, and delete every getActiveConnectedAccount call from run-scoped paths.

#### `web-ios-contract-drift` — Hand-mirrored Swift decoders drift from route payloads with no contract test — live stop-losses render as em-dashes
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort M** · 11 findings: `api-contract-web-ios:api-01`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-06`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-13`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-14`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-16`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-04`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-18`, `performance-accounting:perf-13`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-05`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-11`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-17`
- **Root cause** FullPolicy decodes stopLossPct/trailingStopPct/shortStopLossPct from top-level CodingKeys (ios/SocraticTrade/DeskModels.swift:383-388) while GET /api/policy returns them nested under riskRules (src/lib/types.ts:1188, route at app/api/policy/route.ts:42), so every iPhone Guardrails view shows em-dashes for a configured 8% stop. Nothing ties the two sides together: app/api/mobile/snapshot/route.ts:16-54 is a bare object literal, MobileModels.swift:41-67 decodes every list with `?? []` so a rename blanks a screen silently, and the only Swift decode test is a hand-authored fixture (MobileModelsTests.swift:409). The same class drops recordedAt, benchmarkUnavailable and benchmarkSymbol.
- **The one change** Emit a shared fixture from the real routes (snapshot + policy JSON) and decode it in both `npm test` and the XCTest suite, failing on absent or unknown keys — starting by flattening riskRules in app/api/policy/route.ts:42 or decoding the nested container in DeskModels.swift:372.

#### `approval-path-integrity` — Approving is starved by the run lock and the size that gets placed is not the size that was reviewed
- **P2 · cross-surface · effort L** · 6 findings: `trading-money-path:tsx-09`, `trading-money-path:tsx-10`, `trading-money-path:tsx-11`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-06`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-05`, `alerts-04`
- **Root cause** executeProposal contends for the whole-run strategy lock (src/lib/strategy-execution.ts:297), so an approval during a 15-20 minute autonomous run returns busy — the console gives up after ~2 min and iOS shows a green check. Mobile approvals also queue FIFO behind strategy.run_once because they are absent from IMMEDIATE_MOBILE_COMMAND_TYPES (src/lib/mobile-api.ts:88), whose claim loop is globally ordered with no per-user partition. On the sizing side, approval-time ratio re-anchoring moves EXIT limits with the market (src/lib/approval-reprice.ts:100), a hard-coded 5% headroom shrinks the owner's own cap (src/lib/policy.ts:567), and Retry Red Team stamps approve-at-half without applying the h…
- **The one change** Give approvals a per-account lease instead of the whole-run lock at src/lib/strategy-execution.ts:297 (and add the non-broker command types to the immediate set at mobile-api.ts:88), and make the reprice, haircut and headroom rules apply to the order that is actually placed rather than to the displayed card.

#### `pnl-basis-labels` — Performance tiles print numbers without the basis or window they were computed on
- **P2 · cross-surface · effort M** · 7 findings: `performance-accounting:perf-04`, `performance-accounting:perf-05`, `performance-accounting:perf-06`, `performance-accounting:perf-15`, `performance-accounting:perf-18`, `performance-accounting:perf-17`, `performance-accounting:perf-19`
- **Root cause** src/lib/performance.ts:497 caps the "Versus the market" TWR at ~100 snapshots (≈7 trading days) while app/console/results/page.tsx frames it as account performance; "Realized/Unrealized P&L" means lot-ledger on Results, broker cost on Home and broker positions on iOS with no label on any of them (results/page.tsx:957-994); win rate 0% and avg return +0.00% render for accounts with zero closed lots; app/console/lib/derive.ts:804 divides by NET signed cost basis so shorts inflate the percentage; and performance.ts:1664 fabricates a $100 paper equity base that is rendered as money.
- **The one change** Attach an explicit basis + window label (and an em-dash empty state instead of 0%) to every performance figure at app/console/results/page.tsx:940-1000, ios/SocraticTrade/ResultsView.swift and app/console/lib/derive.ts:804, sourced from the same struct that computed the number.

#### `prompt-trust-boundary` — Non-owner content can reach the always-trusted strategy prompt with no human and a scanner that is inert in production
- **P2 · backend · effort M** · 5 findings: `llm-agent-architecture:llm-04`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-13`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-18`, `coach-05`, `coach-06`
- **Root cause** src/lib/learning-review.ts:640-663 applies any 'keep' verdict with no riskTier exclusion, and applyApprovedPending (src/lib/learned-context/store.ts:397-405) merges strategy-directive rows straight into the strategy prompt — the one region the app never scans (src/lib/prompt-safety.ts:224) — while learningReviewMode defaults to 'decide' (src/lib/defaults.ts:90). Candidates get there from src/lib/chat/coach-learning.ts:567, which stores 480 chars of arbitrary fetched page text as auto-write 'fact' tier, past a classifier scoped to financial-risk vocabulary; the second-layer semantic gate resolves MockLLM in production (src/lib/learned-context/semantic-gate.ts:124); and app/api/chat-history/r…
- **The one change** Apply the injection scan at ingest rather than only at prompt assembly, and require a human for anything that lands in the strategy prompt regardless of review mode — src/lib/chat/coach-learning.ts:567, src/lib/learning-review.ts:660, src/lib/learned-context/store.ts:401 — plus delete the unused POST writer at app/api/chat-history/route.ts:28.

#### `realized-pnl-ledger` — Realized-P&L ledger reads the oldest fills and mismatches lots, so P&L, win rate, tax and wash-sale locks are wrong
- **P2 · backend · effort L** · 9 findings: `performance-accounting:perf-01`, `backend-reliability:berel-02`, `performance-accounting:perf-07`, `performance-accounting:perf-09`, `performance-accounting:perf-10`, `performance-accounting:perf-11`, `performance-accounting:perf-12`, `performance-accounting:perf-21`, `performance-accounting:perf-20`
- **Root cause** src/lib/db-fills.ts:332 is SELECT ... ORDER BY filled_at ASC LIMIT ? — the OLDEST N rows — while the sibling listPortfolioSnapshots (db-fills.ts:221-243) was already fixed to a newest-DESC subquery for this exact bug; all 16 callers pass 500 and src/lib/strategy-tuning.ts:418 passes 30, so the paid tuner's "recentFills" are provably the account's first 20 fills. The matcher on top is also wrong: closes with no app lot are silently dropped (src/lib/performance.ts:599), a lot is graded on its first partial exit only (src/lib/outcome-engine.ts:966), partial closes count as round trips (performance.ts:1707), short-side lots are skipped in tax (src/lib/tax.ts:352), a synthetic 20bps haircut is a…
- **The one change** Fix the read to the newest-DESC subquery already used at db-fills.ts:221-243 and make the lot matcher round-trip aware in src/lib/performance.ts:599-1707 (group partials per lot, book unmatched closes into an explicit pre-app bucket, include short lots in tax, drop the synthetic haircut).

### Tranche 2 — Reliability, Gates, Daily-Use Surfaces (16)

#### `copy-claims-and-rulings` — Shipped copy asserts guardrails the engine does not have and reintroduces the paper/live ceremony the owner ruled out
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort M** · 20 findings: `ux-copy-editor:copy-03`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-11`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-12`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-13`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-16`, `pages-07`, `public-site-growth:dpub-03`, `public-site-growth:dpub-04`, `public-site-growth:dpub-05`, `public-site-growth:dpub-12`, `public-site-growth:dpub-18`, `public-site-growth:dpub-19`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-19`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-19` …
- **Root cause** app/console/macro/indicators.ts:698/706 and again at :753 tell the owner that below-median buys are "hard-vetoed" in risk-off/crisis regimes, while src/lib/strategy-risk.ts:187-197 keeps and tags them (its own comment reads 'advisory pre-veto (tag-not-drop)') and socraticOverrideMode defaults to 'execute'; app/how-it-works/page.tsx:94 lists delegation controls that do not exist and app/framework/content.ts:226 states a sizing invariant the override path contradicts. The same class of copy shouts "(NOT Real Money)" / red Live pills (app/console/settings/brokers.tsx:626, ios/SocraticTrade/ProposalsView.swift:366) and adds an iOS-only confirm for live activation the web does not have.
- **The one change** Run one accuracy pass that rewrites every guardrail claim to the engine's actual advisory behavior and strips the paper/live ceremony, sourcing the guardrail-semantics sentences from a single shared constant so console, iOS and the public pages cannot drift apart again.

#### `dead-controls` — Shipped controls whose backend does nothing, or something other than the label claims
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort M** · 7 findings: `pages-02`, `pages-03`, `pages-05`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-07`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-20`, `admin-03`, `admin-06`
- **Root cause** policy.taxSettings.subtractFromResults is written by app/console/strategy/tax-settings.tsx:213 and read by nothing in the Results render path (app/console/results/page.tsx:940-994 renders P&L unmodified), so "Show results net of estimated tax" changes no number in either direction. The Webhook channel's copy promises Discord embeds that only the separate legacy webhookUrl path builds (app/console/settings/delivery.tsx:37 vs src/lib/notify.ts:262 and src/lib/notifications.ts:330-372), and "Send test" never probes that legacy field. Presets has no create/rename/delete although /api/profiles implements POST/PUT/DELETE; the admin nav omits learning-ledger revert and the RAG backfills entirely;…
- **The one change** For each control, either wire it to the behavior its label claims or remove it — starting with subtractFromResults in app/console/results/page.tsx:940-994, the legacy webhook probe in /api/notifications/test, and preset CRUD against the existing /api/profiles routes.

#### `merge-gate-blindspots` — The required merge gate never compiles or runs the iOS app, and can be skipped entirely for build-imported files
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort M** · 5 findings: `qa-test-strategy:qa-01`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-02`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-03`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-11`, `ios-engineering:ios-09`
- **Root cause** .github/workflows/ci.yml:305's required `verify` job depends only on classify + verify-hosted and never on ios-build.yml, which itself only runs `xcodebuild build` (.github/workflows/ios-build.yml:70-78) — `xcodebuild test` appears nowhere in .github/ or scripts/, so 15 XCTest files covering order-cancel and price-review logic never execute before TestFlight. ci.yml:141's classify regex treats all of docs/** as documentation even though app/api/llm-usage/model-stats/route.ts:9/19 imports docs/benchmarks/*.json at build time, so such an edit auto-merges with tsc/test/build skipped and breaks first in the production deploy. scripts/land.sh:156 runs the same trio and never compiles Swift.
- **The one change** Add an `xcodebuild test` job, make the Swift job a required context in the main-protection ruleset next to `verify`, and exclude build-imported paths from the docs-only regex at ci.yml:141 (mirrored in e2e.yml:77 and scripts/land.sh:156).

#### `phone-touch-viewport` — The phone build inherits desktop control sizes and ignores the visual viewport
- **P1 · mobile-web · effort M** · 15 findings: `mobile-web-ux:mweb-01`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-05`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-06`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-07`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-08`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-09`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-10`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-12`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-13`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-16`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-18`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-19`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-21`, `web-accessibility:a11y-13` …
- **Root cause** app/console/components/chrome.tsx:326 renders the full 'Stop Agent'/'Resume Agent' label in a flex-shrink:0 button (console.css:471-484) alongside a non-shrinking StateChip, avatar and ⌘K trigger, so the only min-w-0 sibling — the account scope selector, the control naming which brokerage account is loaded — collapses at 360-390px; the file's own comment computed its ~73px budget against the older short STOP label. The coarse-pointer 44px floor covers only .con-btn (console.css:458), overlays neither lock scroll nor size with dvh (console.css:858, :1220), inputs at 15px/11px trigger iOS auto-zoom (console.css:1251), and sheets ignore browser history.
- **The one change** Apply the coarse-pointer 44px floor and a 16px input minimum to every interactive class rather than just .con-btn, truncate or icon-ify the chrome run-state label below the sm breakpoint, and give every overlay scroll-lock plus dvh/visualViewport sizing and a history entry.

#### `signin-return-first-run` — Entry paths lose their destination or dead-end: expired sessions, OAuth errors, and iOS first-run
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort M** · 11 findings: `public-site-growth:dpub-01`, `public-site-growth:dpub-02`, `public-site-growth:dpub-17`, `lifecycle-03`, `lifecycle-04`, `lifecycle-05`, `lifecycle-06`, `lifecycle-09`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-09`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-14`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-16`
- **Root cause** middleware.ts:412-419 answers an unauthenticated /api/* with a bare 401 and drops the destination on page redirects, so a push deep link lands on the dashboard after sign-in and app/console/lib/useConsoleData.tsx:139-150 treats that 401 as any transient error — the tab polls stale data forever and Run once reports a generic 'The run failed'. Every Auth.js error routes to /access-denied (src/lib/auth/auth.ts:197) with no way out, and /login renders for already-authenticated users. On iOS the first-run checklist says to connect a broker 'in Account & Settings' (ios/SocraticTrade/HomeView.swift:87) while that very screen's empty state says to use the website (:844), and readiness ignores the L…
- **The one change** Carry the destination through sign-in and special-case 401 in the console fetch client (stop polling, route to /login), and make the iOS first-run checklist either open the AdminPortalView cookie-sharing web view or state plainly that connecting happens on the website.

#### `visual-tokens-theme-contrast` — Tone tokens fail WCAG AA at the sizes they are used, and light-default is not the default in several hosts
- **P1 · cross-surface · effort M** · 14 findings: `web-accessibility:a11y-02`, `web-accessibility:a11y-15`, `web-accessibility:a11y-17`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-25`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-07`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-02`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-03`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-04`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-13`, `public-site-growth:dpub-15`, `frontend-performance:dweb-07`, `frontend-performance:dweb-17`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-13`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-18`
- **Root cause** app/console/console.css:40-73 defines tone text on soft fills that measures 3.68-4.34:1 (independently recomputed) at 11px/12.5px — below the 4.5:1 bar since neither size is 'large text' — including the Stop/Start Agent control; the Approve button's LIVE tag is 9.5px (console.css:578) and input borders are 1.5:1 (console.css:612). iOS uses system Color.green/Color.orange (ios/SocraticTrade/AppComponents.swift:13) and requests Lato weights the bundle does not ship. On the theme side, 'System' is stored by deleting the key so it reads back Light (app/console/lib/useConsoleTheme.ts:37), ⌘K's toggle writes a different hook instance, and <Toaster theme="system"> (app/layout.tsx:80) plus TickerLo…
- **The one change** Darken the tone tokens to at least 4.5:1 at the app's own micro sizes (open PR #2795 already does the console half) and make one theme resolver the single source for the console, the toast host, TickerLogo and iOS so light is the shipped default everywhere.

#### `admin-honesty` — Operator surfaces present placeholders, unattributed writes and mislabeled scope as fact
- **P2 · desktop-web · effort M** · 10 findings: `ops-observability-admin:sre-06`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-09`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-13`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-17`, `admin-01`, `admin-02`, `admin-04`, `admin-05`, `admin-07`, `admin-08`
- **Root cause** app/admin/page.tsx:448 renders a bare CPU percentage with no caveat although app/api/admin/server-metrics/route.ts:604-613 documents the scaling as unverified and potentially an 8x under-report (the detail page at server-metrics-client.tsx:175 carries the caveat), and substitutes 0.0% / '0 Running' when a probe fails. app/api/admin/server-knobs/route.ts:80 audits every knob write as user 'local' regardless of which admin flipped it, and the admin chrome never shows the signed-in identity (app/admin/layout.tsx:217 reads only the avatar image). /admin/transcript calls /api/chat-history, which has no requireAdmin and is scoped to the caller's own turns, while the nav calls it 'every chat turn'…
- **The one change** Carry the caveat and the unknown state through to the Overview card instead of substituting zeros, record and display the real admin identity on knob writes, and put one requireAdmin gate on the /admin page tree plus an admin-scoped transcript read.

#### `alert-push-delivery` — A notification's lifecycle ends before delivery is known, and the tap lands on the wrong screen
- **P2 · cross-surface · effort M** · 11 findings: `alerts-03`, `alerts-07`, `alerts-08`, `alerts-10`, `alerts-11`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-10`, `ios-engineering:ios-05`, `ios-engineering:ios-06`, `ios-engineering:ios-16`, `ios-engineering:ios-17`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-16`
- **Root cause** src/lib/alerts.ts:88-89 flips the alert to the one-shot 'triggered' state before sendNotification is awaited, and sendNotification never throws on total delivery failure (src/lib/notifications.ts:265-279 folds every error into a returned status) — so a revoked token or zero configured channels loses the only chance to notify. The row is tagged with whatever account happened to be active (notifications.ts:216) so it can drop out of the account-filtered Activity feed. The server builds a symbol-specific deep link (src/lib/push-deep-links.ts:99) that neither client reads (app/console/watchlist/page.tsx has no useSearchParams; ios/SocraticTrade/DeepLink.swift:75-77 discards the query), unlike t…
- **The one change** Make delivery the state transition — mark an alert triggered only once a channel accepted it, retrying otherwise — and implement symbol-aware routing plus badge/category/thread-id on both clients, mirroring the proposal deep-link that already works.

#### `auth-session-hardening` — Sessions are unrevocable 30-day bearer tokens behind a soft perimeter
- **P2 · cross-surface · effort L** · 13 findings: `security-auth:sec-02`, `security-auth:sec-03`, `security-auth:sec-04`, `security-auth:sec-05`, `security-auth:sec-06`, `security-auth:sec-09`, `security-auth:sec-10`, `security-auth:sec-11`, `security-auth:sec-12`, `security-auth:sec-13`, `security-auth:sec-14`, `ios-engineering:ios-02`, `ios-engineering:ios-18`
- **Root cause** src/lib/auth/session-token.ts:6 issues a stateless 30-day JWT with no server-side record, so sign-out is a cookie delete and there is no revocation, no 'sign out everywhere' and no absolute lifetime; iOS then holds that cookie in non-Keychain storage with no app lock. The handoff around it is loose: app/api/mobile/auth-redirect/route.ts:24 mints a session code for any authenticated GET with a caller-supplied code_challenge, app/api/mobile/auth/apple/route.ts:23 uses no nonce, and app/api/mobile/push/register/route.ts:84 reassigns another user's device token. The origin answers directly at <PROD_ORIGIN_IP>:443 with Host: socratictrade.com (middleware.ts:173), so the WAF, bot rules and IP trust…
- **The one change** Back sessions with a server-side record (id in the JWT, revocable, absolute lifetime) and bind the mobile handoff to that record with a nonce and a client-bound challenge, then restrict the origin to Cloudflare IPs so the edge rules become load-bearing.

#### `broker-order-shape` — Each broker adapter rewrites the approved order differently and records nothing
- **P2 · backend · effort M** · 6 findings: `trading-money-path:tsx-06`, `trading-money-path:tsx-07`, `trading-money-path:tsx-13`, `trading-money-path:tsx-16`, `trading-money-path:tsx-18`, `trading-money-path:tsx-19`
- **Root cause** There is no shared venue-normalization step: src/lib/alpaca.ts:157 keeps time_in_force=gtc on extended-hours limits that Alpaca rejects with a 422, alpaca.ts:741 never forwards extended_hours on the MCP path, src/lib/robinhood.ts:972 silently converts a fractional or extended-hours limit into a regular-hours MARKET order while the persisted proposal and fill receipt still say 'limit', src/lib/tradier.ts:789 sends the unrounded limit price and always attaches a stop field, getEquityTradability is a stub returning tradable/fractional true for every symbol (alpaca.ts:582), and the hard-gate copy names Robinhood for Alpaca accounts (src/lib/policy.ts:326).
- **The one change** Put one venue-normalization step in front of every adapter's place call that resolves TIF, extended-hours, rounding and fractional support per broker and audits any rewrite, and make the tradability probe real so the pre-flight check means something.

#### `console-a11y-keyboard` — Shared console overlay and menu primitives skip the keyboard and announcement contract
- **P2 · desktop-web · effort M** · 8 findings: `web-accessibility:a11y-03`, `web-accessibility:a11y-05`, `web-accessibility:a11y-06`, `web-accessibility:a11y-08`, `web-accessibility:a11y-09`, `web-accessibility:a11y-10`, `web-accessibility:a11y-16`, `web-accessibility:a11y-20`
- **Root cause** The primitives every page composes are non-conformant: app/console/ui/sheet.tsx:116's focus sentry and Escape handler are not stack-aware, so Escape on a drawer or palette above a Sheet closes the Sheet underneath; app/console/components/shell.tsx:180 has no skip link past ~22 focus stops; app/console/ui/toast.tsx:38 announces errors politely and auto-dismisses in 9s with no hover/focus pause, and typed-confirm server refusals are never announced at all; approval cards have no accessible name and repeat a bare 'Reject' (approval-card.tsx:455); the account-scope menu uses role=menu with no arrow-key handling plus an invisible full-screen focusable button (chrome.tsx:235).
- **The one change** Fix the shared primitives once — an overlay stack for focus/Escape in sheet.tsx, assertive non-auto-dismissing error toasts, accessible names on menu/dialog/card roots and a skip link in shell.tsx — so all 20+ call sites inherit the behavior.

#### `console-ia-forms-blotters` — Console pages disagree about where things live, how they save, and how they are searched
- **P2 · desktop-web · effort L** · 15 findings: `desktop-web-ux:dweb-02`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-03`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-04`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-05`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-06`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-08`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-09`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-10`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-11`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-17`, `pages-08`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-20`, `desktop-web-ux:dweb-20`, `performance-accounting:perf-23` …
- **Root cause** Three different save models coexist across the Configure pages (app/console/guardrails/page.tsx:335), and two numeric fields PATCH on every keystroke against the blur-commit pattern every sibling uses (app/console/settings/page.tsx:874 and strategy/overlays-panel.tsx:170, where a mid-edit empty field saves the fallback 2). /console/decisions is orphaned from the nav (decisions/page.tsx:1), the proposal lifecycle spans five surfaces, a bare 'r' key fires a strategy run with two mounted RunOnce buttons (command-palette.tsx:128), blotters cannot be searched, filtered or exported (activity/page.tsx:216), the Positions blotter lives only on Home without last price or exit action, and Results has…
- **The one change** Standardize the page contract — one save model (local draft plus explicit commit), one hotkey/verb registry, one blotter component with search/filter/export — and put every route in the nav section it belongs to.

#### `ios-network-decode` — One transient response or one bad row degrades the whole iOS app
- **P2 · ios · effort M** · 7 findings: `ios-engineering:ios-03`, `ios-engineering:ios-08`, `ios-engineering:ios-12`, `ios-engineering:ios-13`, `ios-engineering:ios-21`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-07`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-09`
- **Root cause** A single failed background reload flips snapshotLoadFailed and the 3-minute staleness gate then disables Approve/Run/Watchlist/Policy (ios/SocraticTrade/MobileStore.swift:223) with no polling fallback; GETs have no 5xx retry and the Cloudflare mapping stops at 523 (MobileAPIClient.swift:373), so deploy-swap 502/503s reach the user raw. SSE reconnects on a fixed 5s loop and every scene-active tears down a healthy stream (MobileStore.swift:343), while every SSE frame triggers a full snapshot reload. Decoding is all-or-nothing: one null required field inside any list element fails the entire snapshot (MobileModels.swift:240) and duplicate command ids trap outright (MobileStore.swift:46).
- **The one change** Make decoding element-tolerant — drop and count bad rows instead of failing the payload — and add bounded retry with backoff for idempotent GETs and SSE, so a deploy swap or one malformed row degrades a section rather than the app.

#### `llm-catalog-routing-cost` — Model choice, receipts and cost each derive from a stale or partial source
- **P2 · cross-surface · effort M** · 11 findings: `llm-agent-architecture:llm-03`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-07`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-09`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-10`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-11`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-12`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-14`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-15`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-16`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-17`, `llm-agent-architecture:llm-20`
- **Root cause** src/lib/model-rotation.ts:73 hardcodes a 'dead' OpenRouter list that drops slugs the live catalog still serves; app/console/components/approval-card.tsx:127 asserts 'this run's rotation pick, not a failover' even when an implicit fallback answered; src/lib/chat/llm.ts:655 routes Coach to native providers while the engine and the save-gate assume universal OpenRouter; late post-soft-timeout responses are audited but never metered (src/lib/strategy.ts:6594) and OpenRouter's billed usage.cost is never requested or stored (src/lib/llm-usage.ts:180), so /console/usage is derived from a hand-maintained price table with no embedding/rerank row; and Red Team re-sends the entire Green user content (…
- **The one change** Make the live OpenRouter catalog plus the response's own billed cost the source of truth for rotation, receipts and the Usage page, and compact the Red Team payload to the documented RedTeamReviewContext subset.

#### `ops-liveness-durability` — Nothing pages when the desk stops, and the backup/disk story is unverified
- **P2 · cross-surface · effort L** · 15 findings: `ops-observability-admin:sre-01`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-02`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-03`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-04`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-05`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-07`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-08`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-11`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-14`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-15`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-16`, `backend-reliability:berel-05`, `backend-reliability:berel-13`, `backend-reliability:berel-14` …
- **Root cause** app/api/health/route.ts:88 and :128 set checks.schedulerStale and checks.tradingLiveness.degraded without changing the 200 (ok flips only at :78 and :279), and nothing pushes on them — src/lib/scheduler.ts has a single sendNotification call, line 372 — so a wedged scheduler is discoverable only by an external keyword monitor. The Docker HEALTHCHECK gives that same heavy route 5s (Dockerfile:75) while documented stalls exceed it. The R2 'writes paused' banner reads a leftover marker with no endpoint check (src/lib/r2-usage.ts:571 vs scripts/coolify-prod-start.sh:200-212, live replica is B2), nothing rotates litestream-runtime.log (coolify-prod-start.sh:223), no table has retention (src/lib/d…
- **The one change** Make liveness a first-class push plus one operator card — fire a notification on schedulerStale/tradingLivenessDegraded, split a cheap liveness route for the container probe, gate the R2 banner on isLitestreamReplicaCloudflareR2(), and add log rotation plus table retention.

#### `test-coverage-ci-cost` — The suite covers shapes but not money paths, reaches the live internet, and serializes 14 minutes into every run
- **P2 · backend · effort L** · 12 findings: `qa-test-strategy:qa-05`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-06`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-07`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-08`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-09`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-10`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-12`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-17`, `ios-engineering:ios-22`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-13`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-15`, `qa-test-strategy:qa-16`
- **Root cause** vitest.config.ts has no setupFiles at all — only a globalSetup that plumbs TMPDIR — so nothing prevents a test from reaching SiliconFlow, SEC or Yahoo (STATUS.md still records those 404s), and maxWorkers:1 serializes ~7,000 tests into ~14 minutes of every CI run. 63 of 122 routes have no route-level test including single-proposal approve (app/api/proposals/[id]/approve/route.ts:11), TestBrokerGateway always fills instantly (src/lib/robinhood.ts:920) so only the happy path is reachable, MobileStore.submit/load are untested despite an existing URLProtocol harness, there is no coverage tooling, and the codex-autofix workflow fails on every review event.
- **The one change** Add a vitest setup file that fails any un-mocked outbound fetch plus a partial-fill/reject mode on TestBrokerGateway, then write route tests for approve/replace/pause and re-enable parallel workers behind the per-worker temp DB the config already provisions.

### Tranche 3 — Structure, Polish, Owner Decisions (7)

#### `a11y-structure-naming` — Document structure and accessible names are stylistic rather than semantic
- **P2 · cross-surface · effort M** · 7 findings: `web-accessibility:a11y-04`, `web-accessibility:a11y-07`, `web-accessibility:a11y-11`, `web-accessibility:a11y-12`, `web-accessibility:a11y-14`, `web-accessibility:a11y-18`, `web-accessibility:a11y-19`
- **Root cause** Every console route shares the document title 'Socratic Trade' (app/console/layout.tsx:5, WCAG 2.4.2); 68 heading-styled divs carry con-card-title against 16 real h2/h3 and the collapsible Card demotes its h2 to a span (ui/primitives.tsx:35); none of the 23 console tables has a caption or aria-label and 105 of 106 th elements lack scope (components/positions.tsx:89); the Meter progressbar has no accessible name at any call site (primitives.tsx:201); roughly 147 explanations live only in native title= on non-interactive elements (primitives.tsx:485); readiness state is conveyed by icon and border color alone (readiness-checklist.tsx:27); and the login page has no heading.
- **The one change** Fix the primitives and the layout metadata once — per-route titles, real heading elements inside Card, caption/scope in the table primitive, names on Meter and Tooltip — so every page inherits the structure.

#### `console-first-paint-bundle` — First console paint waits on a large bundle and then a single blocking snapshot
- **P2 · desktop-web · effort M** · 7 findings: `frontend-performance:dweb-06`, `frontend-performance:dweb-08`, `frontend-performance:dweb-09`, `frontend-performance:dweb-10`, `frontend-performance:dweb-11`, `frontend-performance:dweb-12`, `frontend-performance:dweb-13`
- **Root cause** app/console/layout.tsx:22 makes HTML → ~1.5 MB raw JS → /api/dashboard a strict waterfall, and routes that fetch their own data still wait behind the full-screen snapshot loader (components/shell.tsx:67). The single console context re-renders every page three times per poll and once per SSE event because fetching and stream health share the context value with snapshot (lib/useConsoleData.tsx:341). Sentry Session Replay (~38 KB gz) ships although replay is env-disabled (instrumentation-client.ts:31), framer motion (~40 KB gz) loads on every page for one tooltip fade (ui/primitives.tsx:7), three heavy client libraries are declared but never imported, and a 1 MB PNG is the primary favicon.
- **The one change** Split the context so data and transport health do not re-render together, drop the disabled Replay bundle, the motion dependency and the unused libraries, and let self-fetching routes paint before the snapshot resolves.

#### `copy-consistency-rules` — Owner copy rules are unenforced on the web: two-space gaps, Title Case, money format, Central time
- **P2 · cross-surface · effort M** · 21 findings: `ux-copy-editor:copy-01`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-02`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-05`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-06`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-07`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-08`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-09`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-10`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-15`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-17`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-18`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-19`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-21`, `ux-copy-editor:copy-22` …
- **Root cause** SENTENCE_GAP is used in 9 files against roughly 600 web copy strings (app/console/lib/format.ts:54), and the double ASCII spaces typed into JSX collapse to one at render (app/console/components/consent-gate.tsx:107) so source looks compliant and the screen is not; public pages have zero NBSP gaps. Headings and buttons are sentence case against the Title Case rule and against iOS (chrome.tsx:789), one run state carries three names and one stop control four (derive.ts:171), the same guardrail is labeled differently on web and iOS (GuardrailsView.swift:69), compact money renders uppercase suffixes (drilldown-data.ts:674), and app/console/settings/legal.tsx:40 calls toLocaleString() with no tim…
- **The one change** Add the copy-rule lint the iOS suite already has — two-space gap, Title Case headings and buttons, lowercase compact money, Central-time timestamps, one term per concept — and run a single pass to make the strings pass it.

#### `ios-build-compliance` — iOS project-level hygiene: no privacy manifest, hardcoded production origin, no strict concurrency
- **P2 · ios · effort M** · 5 findings: `ios-engineering:ios-10`, `ios-engineering:ios-11`, `ios-engineering:ios-19`, `ios-engineering:ios-20`, `api-contract-web-ios:api-18`
- **Root cause** ios/project.yml:39 ships no PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy although the app uses UserDefaults, a required-reason API — ITMS-91053 on upload; the production origin is hardcoded in LoginView.swift:265, AdminPortalView and DeepLink with no debug override, so every simulator run hits production despite MobileAPIClient.productionBaseURL existing to prevent that drift; project.yml:18 builds in Swift 5.9 language mode with no strict-concurrency checking; and MobileStore/PushNotificationCoordinator/TabPreferences are ObservableObject against the repo's own @Observable rule, re-publishing on every SSE line.
- **The one change** One ios/project.yml pass — add the privacy manifest, a build-configuration-driven base URL and strict concurrency — plus the @Observable migration the repo rule already requires.

#### `ios-parity-gaps` — iOS renders a read-only subset of the screens it mirrors
- **P2 · ios · effort L** · 11 findings: `ios-hig-ux:ios-02`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-03`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-04`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-06`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-10`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-15`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-19`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-20`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-21`, `ios-hig-ux:ios-22`, `performance-accounting:perf-24`

- **The one change** Close the parity list against the console screens these mirror — a shared markdown renderer, the decision-journal feed, per-position P&L and an equity curve — and surface Account & Settings from More as well as Home.

#### `phone-layout-density` — Phone layouts are the desktop tree at desktop density
- **P2 · mobile-web · effort M** · 9 findings: `mobile-web-ux:mweb-11`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-14`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-15`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-17`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-20`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-22`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-23`, `frontend-performance:dweb-14`, `frontend-performance:dweb-16`
- **Root cause** app/console/console.css:90 defines no phone type-scale bump, so 412 uses of 11px and 185 of 12.5px render at desktop sizes on a 6-inch screen; /console/watchlist keeps a 5-column table whose row actions scroll off (watchlist/page.tsx:227); Home buries portfolio value and day P&L under the readiness hero and strategy bar (page.tsx:217); Scan's phone branch is an unsorted, unfilterable, unvirtualized card wall mounted alongside the hidden desktop tree (scan-table.tsx:505); the equity chart scales to ~70px tall (equity-chart.tsx:11); the Coach composer's Enter always sends with no way to type a newline; and two full-viewport animated gradient orbs keep animating under the opaque console (app/g…

#### `public-site-funnel-seo` — The public site's two halves do not connect and its metadata is stale
- **P3 · public-site · effort M** · 11 findings: `public-site-growth:dpub-07`, `public-site-growth:dpub-08`, `public-site-growth:dpub-09`, `public-site-growth:dpub-10`, `public-site-growth:dpub-11`, `public-site-growth:dpub-13`, `public-site-growth:dpub-14`, `public-site-growth:dpub-16`, `public-site-growth:dpub-20`, `mobile-web-ux:mweb-24`, `ops-observability-admin:sre-19`

Metadata is stale or overridden: the sitemap still advertises the deleted /design/socratic-trade route (app/sitemap.ts:15), Cloudflare's managed Content Signals robots prepends Allow: / ahead of the app's Disallow: / (app/robots.ts:47), the root canonical '/' is inherited by every page that does not override it (app/layout.tsx:41), summary_large_image is declared with no image,…
- **The one change** Connect the funnel — Sign in on /welcome, an explainer link on /login, one canonical explainer page — and correct the metadata: per-page canonical, accurate sitemap entries, an OG image, and a robots policy that matches what is actually served.

*On what was left out of clusters:* No finding was left as an orphan: all 384 in-scope items (334 register findings after excluding the two ALREADY_FIXED ones, plus the 50 gap-lane findings) are assigned to exactly one of the 45 clusters, with zero duplicates and zero uncovered P1/P2 members (37 P1 + 178 P2 after applying the second-round severity corrections). ## D. Implementation Plans For Tranche 1 (22)

Written against current code so another agent can execute without re-deriving the analysis.  Each names the edit sites, the contract after the change, and the test that must fail first.

### `account-write-guards` (effort M)

## E. Method And What Still Is Not Proven

- **Second round:** 8 batches, one per risk family, each told to refute.  Model tier was matched to the task: the money-path, accounting, backend-reliability and security batches ran on the frontier tier (a wrong call there costs real money); the LLM, market-data, client-surface and process batches ran on the mid tier; the five gap lanes and their skeptics and the plan authors ran on the mid tier.  Result: 27 upheld, 11 narrowed, 2 already fixed, 0 refuted outright.
- **Still not proven:** anything marked UNPROVABLE_FROM_CODE, plus every claim about production data volumes.  The clearest example is the fill-ledger cap (`performance-accounting:perf-01`, `backend-reliability:berel-02`): the code truncates to the OLDEST 500 rows per (account, source), but no one has counted the rows in the production DB, so whether it is biting TODAY is unknown.  One `SELECT source, COUNT(*) FROM fill_events GROUP BY source` per account settles it.
- **Not re-verified:** the P2/P3 tail from Part I kept its first-round verdicts.  Given the second round's hit rate on P1s (28% narrowed or already fixed), assume a similar fraction of the P2/P3 tail is overstated, and re-check before acting on any single one.
- **No production writes** were made in either part.  The only code change this review produced is hotfix #2851, which is live.
